'../types';\nimport { vectorizationConfigService } from './vectorizationConfigService';\n\nconst API_BASE = '/api/processing';\n\nfunction sseProcessingStream(\n  url: string,\n  source: SyncSourceType,\n  onProgress: (progress: ProcessingProgress) => void,\n  onRecordProcessed: (record: ProcessingRecord) => void,\n  signal: AbortSignal,\n  pauseEndpoint: string,\n  extraParams?: Record<string, string>\n): Promise<ProcessingProgress> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, _reject) => {\n    const fullUrl = new URL(url, window.location.origin);\n    if (extraParams) {\n      Object.entries(extraParams).forEach(([k, v]) => fullUrl.searchParams.set(k, v));\n    }\n\n    const eventSource = new EventSource(fullUrl.toString());\n\n    let lastProgress: ProcessingProgress = {\n      source,\n      status: 'processing',\n      totalRecords: 0,\n      processedRecords: 0,\n      successCount: 0,\n      failedCount: 0,\n      skippedCount: 0,\n    };\n\n    eventSource.addEventListener('record', (event) => {\n      const record: ProcessingRecord = JSON.parse(event.data);\n      onRecordProcessed(record);\n    });\n\n    eventSource.addEventListener('progress', (event) => {\n      lastProgress = JSON.parse(event.data);\n      onProgress(lastProgress);\n    });\n\n    eventSource.addEventListener('complete', (event) => {\n      const finalProgress: ProcessingProgress = JSON.parse(event.data);\n      eventSource.close();\n      resolve(finalProgress);\n    });\n\n    eventSource.addEventListener('error', () => {\n      eventSource.close();\n      resolve({ ...lastProgress, status: 'error' });\n    });\n\n    signal.addEventListener('abort', () => {\n      eventSource.close();\n      fetch(pauseEndpoint, { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => {});\n      resolve({ ...lastProgress, status: 'paused' });\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nexport const resumeProcessingService = {\n  async fetchProcessingStatus(source: SyncSourceType): Promise<{\n    totalEligible: number;\n    alreadyProcessed: number;\n  }> {\n    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/status/${source}`);\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch processing status: ${res.status}`);\n    return res.json();\n  },\n\n  startExtraction(\n    source: SyncSourceType,\n    token: string,\n    onProgress: (progress: ProcessingProgress) => void,\n    onRecordProcessed: (record: ProcessingRecord) => void,\n    signal: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<ProcessingProgress> {\n    return sseProcessingStream(\n      `${API_BASE}/extract-stream/${source}?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`,\n      source,\n      onProgress,\n      onRecordProcessed,\n      signal,\n      `${API_BASE}/extract-pause`\n    );\n  },\n\n  startVectorization(\n    source: SyncSourceType,\n    onProgress: (progress: ProcessingProgress) => void,\n    onRecordProcessed: (record: ProcessingRecord) => void,\n    signal: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<ProcessingProgress> {\n    const vecConfig = vectorizationConfigService.getConfig();\n    return sseProcessingStream(\n      `${API_BASE}/vectorize-stream/${source}`,\n      source,\n      onProgress,\n      onRecordProcessed,\n      signal,\n      `${API_BASE}/vectorize-pause`,\n      { model: vecConfig.model }\n    );\n  },\n\n  async retryFailed(source: SyncSourceType): Promise<{ reset: number }> {\n    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/retry-failed/${source}`, { method: 'POST' });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to retry: ${res.status}`);\n    return res.json();\n  },\n\n  async retryFailedVectorization(source: SyncSourceType): Promise<{ reset: number }> {\n    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/retry-failed-vectorization/${source}`, { method: 'POST' });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to retry vectorization: ${res.status}`);\n    return res.json();\n  },\n\n  async vectorizeSingle(\n    source: SyncSourceType,\n    upstreamId: number\n  ): Promise<ProcessingRecord> {\n    const vecConfig = vectorizationConfigService.getConfig();\n    const res = await fetch(\n from '../shared/ipc-channels';\n\nclass DataSyncApi {\n  async validateToken(token: string): Promise<{ valid: boolean; error?: string }> {\n    return Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['fetchSyncStatus']['response']> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.FETCH_SYNC_STATUS, { source, year });\n  }\n\n  async fetchRecords(source: Parameters<DataSyncIpcTypes['fetchRecords']['request']>[0]['source'], year?: number): Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['fetchRecords']['response']> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.FETCH_RECORDS, { source, year });\n  }\n\n  async clearRecords(source: DataSyncIpcTypes['clearRecords']['request']['source'], year?: number): Promise<void> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.CLEAR_RECORDS, { source, year });\n  }\n\n  async syncSingleRecord(source: Parameters<DataSyncIpcTypes['syncSingleRecord']['request']>[0]['source'], token: string, upstreamId: number): Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['syncSingleRecord']['response']> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.SYNC_SINGLE_RECORD, { source, token, upstreamId });\n  }\n\n  async pauseSync(): Promise<void> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.PAUSE_SYNC);\n  }\n\n  async uploadNote(token: string, personId: number, noteType: string, filePath: string, fileName: string): Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['uploadNote']['response']> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.UPLOAD_NOTE, { token, personId, noteType, filePath, fileName });\n  }\n\n  startSync(\n    params: DataSyncIpcTypes['startSync']['request'],\n    handlers: {\n      onProgress?: (progress: EventPayloads['syncProgress']) => void;\n      onRecordUpdated?: (record: EventPayloads['syncRecordUpdated']) => void;\n      onComplete?: (finalProgress: EventPayloads['syncComplete']) => void;\n      onError?: (error: EventPayloads['syncError']) => void;\n    },\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['startSync']['response']> {\n    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n      const requestId = `sync_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`;\n\n      const cleanup = () => {\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_PROGRESS}_${requestId}`);\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_RECORD_UPDATED}_${requestId}`);\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_COMPLETE}_${requestId}`);\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_ERROR}_${requestId}`);\n      };\n\n      if (handlers.onProgress) {\n        ipcRenderer.on(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_PROGRESS}_${requestId}`, (_, data) => {\n          handlers.onProgress!(data);\n        });\n      }\n\n      if (handlers.onRecordUpdated) {\n const cleanup = () => {\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_PROGRESS}_${requestId}`);\n });\n\n      ipcRenderer.once(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_ERROR}_${requestId}`, (_, error) => {\n        cleanup();\n        if (handlers.onError) handlers.onError(error);\n        reject(new from '../shared/ipc-channels';\n\nclass DataSyncApi {\n  async validateToken(token: string): Promise<{ valid: boolean; error?: string }> {\n    return Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['fetchSyncStatus']['response']> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.FETCH_SYNC_STATUS, { source, year });\n  }\n\n  async fetchRecords(source: Parameters<DataSyncIpcTypes['fetchRecords']['request']>[0]['source'], year?: number): Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['fetchRecords']['response']> {\n    return {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.CLEAR_RECORDS, { source, year });\n  }\n\n  async syncSingleRecord(source: ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.SYNC_SINGLE_RECORD, { source, token, upstreamId });\n  }\n\n  async pauseSync(): Promise<void> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.PAUSE_SYNC);\n  }\n\n  async uploadNote(token: string, personId: number, noteType: string, filePath: string, fileName: string): Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['uploadNote']['response']> {\n    return ipcRenderer.invoke(IPC_CHANNELS.DATA_SYNC.UPLOAD_NOTE, { token, personId, noteType, filePath, fileName });\n  }\n\n  startSync(\n    params: DataSyncIpcTypes['startSync']['request'],\n    handlers: {\n      onProgress?: (progress: EventPayloads['syncProgress']) => void;\n      onRecordUpdated?: (record: EventPayloads['syncRecordUpdated']) => void;\n      onComplete?: (finalProgress: EventPayloads['syncComplete']) => void;\n      onError?: (error: EventPayloads['syncError']) => void;\n    },\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<DataSyncIpcTypes['startSync']['response']> {\n    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n      const requestId = `sync_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`;\n\n      const cleanup = () => {\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_PROGRESS}_${requestId}`);\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_RECORD_UPDATED}_${requestId}`);\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_COMPLETE}_${requestId}`);\n        ipcRenderer.removeAllListeners(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_ERROR}_${requestId}`);\n      };\n\n      if (handlers.onProgress) {\n        ipcRenderer.on(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_PROGRESS}_${requestId}`, (_, data) => {\n          handlers.onProgress!(data);\n        });\n      }\n\n      if (handlers.onRecordUpdated) {\n        ipcRenderer.on(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_RECORD_UPDATED}_${requestId}`, (_, data) => {\n          handlers.onRecordUpdated!(data);\n        });\n      }\n\n      ipcRenderer.once(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_COMPLETE}_${requestId}`, (_, data) => {\n        cleanup();\n        if (handlers.onComplete) handlers.onComplete(data);\n        resolve(data);\n      });\n\n      ipcRenderer.once(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_ERROR}_${requestId}`, (_, error) => {\n        cleanup();\n        if (handlers.onError) handlers.onError(error);\n        reject(new Error(error.error));\n      });\n\n      if (signal) {\n        signal.addEventListener('abort', () => {\n          cleanup();\n          this.pauseSync();\n          resolve({\n            source: ipcRenderer.once(`${IPC_CHANNELS.EVENTS.SYNC_ERROR}_${requestId}`, (_, error) => {\n        cleanup();\n        reject(new Error(error.error));\n      });\n\n      if (signal) {\n 'Upgraded', 'Utilized',\n];\n\nexport const validationService = {\n  validateResume(resume: StructuredResume, template?: ResumeTemplate): ValidationResult[] {\n    const activeTemplate = template || getDefaultTemplate();\n    const results: ValidationResult[] = [];\n\n    results.push(...this.validateSummary(resume.summary, activeTemplate));\n    results.push(...this.validateExperience(resume.experience, activeTemplate));\n    results.push(...this.validateEducation(resume.education, activeTemplate));\n    results.push(...this.validateSkills(resume.skills, activeTemplate));\n    results.push(...this.validateCertifications(resume.certifications, activeTemplate));\n    results.push(...this.validateContactInfo(resume));\n\n    return results;\n  },\n\n  validateSummary(summary: string): ValidationResult[] {\n    const results: ValidationResult[] = [];\n\n    if (!summary || summary.trim().length === 0) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'summary',\n        status: 'error',\n        message: 'Professional summary is required',\n        rule: 'presence',\n      });\n      return results;\n    }\n\n    if (summary.length < 100) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'summary',\n        status: 'warning',\n        message: 'Summary should be at least 100 characters for better impact',\n        rule: 'min-length',\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (summary.length > 500) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'summary',\n        status: 'warning',\n        message: 'Summary exceeds 500 characters. Consider making it more concise.',\n        rule: 'max-length',\n      });\n    }\n\n    const pronounPattern = /\\b(I|me|my|myself)\\b/gi;\n    if (pronounPattern.test(summary)) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'summary',\n        status: 'warning',\n        message: 'Avoid first-person pronouns in professional summary',\n        rule: 'no-pronouns',\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (results.length === 0) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'summary',\n        status: 'valid',\n        message: 'Summary meets all requirements',\n        rule: 'complete',\n      });\n    }\n\n    return results;\n  },\n\n  validateExperience(\n    experience: StructuredResume['experience'],\n    _template: ResumeTemplate\n  ): ValidationResult[] {\n    const results: ValidationResult[] = [];\n\n    if (!experience || experience.length === 0) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'experience',\n        status: 'error',\n        message: 'At least one experience entry is required',\n        rule: 'presence',\n      });\n      return results;\n    }\n\n    experience.forEach((exp, index) => {\n      const prefix = `experience[${index}]`;\n\n      if (!exp.title || exp.title.trim().length === 0) {\n        results.push({\n          field: `${prefix}.title`,\n          status: 'error',\n          message: `Job title is required for experience #${index + 1}`,\n          rule: 'presence',\n        });\n      }\n\n      if (!exp.company || exp.company.trim().length === 0) {\n        results.push({\n          field: `${prefix}.company`,\n          status: 'error',\n          message: `Company name is required for experience #${index + 1}`,\n          rule: 'presence',\n        });\n      }\n\n      if (!exp.startDate) {\n        results.push({\n          field: `${prefix}.dates`,\n          status: 'error',\n          message: `Start date is required for experience #${index + 1}`,\n          rule: 'presence',\n        });\n      }\n\n      if (exp.achievements && exp.achievements.length > 0) {\n        exp.achievements.forEach((achievement, achIndex) => {\n          const firstWord = achievement.trim().split(' ')[0];\n          const isActionVerb = ACTION_VERBS.some(\n            (verb) => verb.toLowerCase() === firstWord.toLowerCase()\n          );\n\n          if (!isActionVerb && achievement.length > 10) {\n            results.push({\n              field: `${prefix}.achievements[${achIndex}]`,\n              status: 'warning',\n              message: `Achievement should start with an action verb (e.g., Led, Developed, Achieved)`,\n              rule: 'action-verbs',\n            });\n          }\n\n          const hasMetrics = /\\d+%|\\$\\d+|\\d+\\+|\\d+x|\\d+ (team|people|users|customers)/i.test(\n            achievement\n          );\n          if (!hasMetrics && achievement.length > 20) {\n            results.push({\n              field: `${prefix}.achievements[${achIndex}]`,\n              status: 'warning',\n              message: 'Consider adding quantified metrics to this achievement',\n              rule: 'quantify',\n            });\n          }\n        });\n      } else if (!exp.description || exp.description.length < 50) {\n        results.push({\n          field: `${prefix}.description`,\n          status: 'warning',\n          message: `Add achievements or a more detailed description for experience #${index + 1}`,\n          rule: 'content-quality',\n        });\n      }\n    });\n\n    const hasNoErrors = !results.some(\n      (r) => r.field.startsWith('experience') && r.status === 'error'\n    );\n    if (hasNoErrors && experience.length > 0) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'experience',\n        status: 'valid',\n        message: 'Experience section is complete',\n        rule: 'complete',\n      });\n    }\n\n    return results;\n  },\n\n  validateEducation(\n    education: StructuredResume['education'],\n    template: ResumeTemplate\n  ): ValidationResult[] {\n    const results: ValidationResult[] = [];\n    const eduSection = template.sections.find((s) => s.type === 'education');\n\n    if (eduSection?.required && (!education || education.length === 0)) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'education',\n        status: 'error',\n        message: 'At least one education entry is required',\n        rule: 'presence',\n      });\n      return results;\n    }\n\n    education?.forEach((edu, index) => {\n      const prefix = `education[${index}]`;\n\n      if (!edu.institution || edu.institution.trim().length === 0) {\n        results.push({\n          field: `${prefix}.institution`,\n          status: 'error',\n          message: `Institution is required for education #${index + 1}`,\n          rule: 'presence',\n        });\n      }\n\n      if (!edu.degree || edu.degree.trim().length === 0) {\n        results.push({\n          field: `${prefix}.degree`,\n          status: 'error',\n          message: `Degree is required for education #${index + 1}`,\n          rule: 'presence',\n        });\n      }\n    });\n\n    const hasNoErrors = !results.some(\n      (r) => r.field.startsWith('education') && r.status === 'error'\n    );\n    if (hasNoErrors && education && education.length > 0) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'education',\n        status: 'valid',\n        message: 'Education section is complete',\n        rule: 'complete',\n      });\n    }\n\n    return results;\n  },\n\n  validateSkills(\n    skills: StructuredResume['skills'],\n    _template: ResumeTemplate\n  ): ValidationResult[] {\n    const results: ValidationResult[] = [];\n\n    if (!skills || skills.length === 0) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'skills',\n        status: 'error',\n        message: 'Skills section is required',\n        rule: 'presence',\n      });\n      return results;\n    }\n\n    const totalSkills = skills.reduce((sum, cat) => sum + cat.skills.length, 0);\n\n    if (totalSkills < 5) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'skills',\n        status: 'warning',\n        message: 'Consider adding more skills (recommended: 8-15)',\n        rule: 'content-quality',\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (totalSkills > 30) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'skills',\n        status: 'warning',\n        message: 'Too many skills listed. Focus on the most relevant ones (recommended: 15-25)',\n        rule: 'content-quality',\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (results.filter((r) => r.field === 'skills' && r.status !== 'valid').length === 0) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'skills',\n        status: 'valid',\n        message: 'Skills section is complete',\n        rule: 'complete',\n      });\n    }\n\n    return results;\n  },\n\n  validateCertifications(\n    certifications: StructuredResume['certifications'],\n    template: ResumeTemplate\n  ): ValidationResult[] {\n    const results: ValidationResult[] = [];\n    const certSection = template.sections.find((s) => s.type === 'certifications');\n\n    if (certSection?.required && (!certifications || certifications.length === 0)) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'certifications',\n        status: 'warning',\n        message: 'Consider adding relevant certifications if available',\n        rule: 'presence',\n      });\n    }\n\n    certifications?.forEach((cert, index) => {\n      const prefix = `certifications[${index}]`;\n\n      if (!cert.name || cert.name.trim().length === 0) {\n        results.push({\n          field: `${prefix}.name`,\n          status: 'error',\n          message: `Certification name is required for entry #${index + 1}`,\n          rule: 'presence',\n        });\n      }\n\n      if (!cert.issuer || cert.issuer.trim().length === 0) {\n        results.push({\n          field: `${prefix}.issuer`,\n          status: 'warning',\n          message: `Issuing organization recommended for certification #${index + 1}`,\n          rule: 'content-quality',\n        });\n      }\n    });\n\n    return results;\n  },\n\n  validateContactInfo(resume: StructuredResume): ValidationResult[] {\n    const results: ValidationResult[] = [];\n\n    if (!resume.candidateName || resume.candidateName.trim().length === 0) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'candidateName',\n        status: 'error',\n        message: 'Candidate name is required',\n        rule: 'presence',\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (!resume.email) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'email',\n        status: 'warning',\n        message: 'Email address is recommended',\n        rule: 'presence',\n      });\n    } else {\n      const emailPattern = /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/;\n      if (!emailPattern.test(resume.email)) {\n        results.push({\n          field: 'email',\n          status: 'error',\n          message: 'Invalid email format',\n          rule: 'format',\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (!resume.phone) {\n      results.push({\n        field: 'phone',\n        status: 'warning',\n        message: 'Phone number is recommended',\n        rule: 'presence',\n      });\n    }\n\n    return results;\n  },\n\n  getOverallStatus(results: ValidationResult[]): ValidationStatus {\n    if (results.some((r) => r.status === 'error')) {\n      return 'error';\n    }\n    if (results.some((r) => r.status === 'warning')) {\n      return 'warning';\n    }\n    if (results.some((r) => r.status === 'valid')) {\n      return 'valid';\n    }\n    return 'pending';\n  },\n\n  getCompleteness(resume: StructuredResume): {\n    percentage: number;\n    filledFields: number;\n    totalFields: number;\n    missingFields: string[];\n  } {\n    const fields = [\n      { name: 'Candidate Name', filled: !!resume.candidateName },\n      { name: 'Email', filled: !!resume.email },\n      { name: 'Phone', filled: !!resume.phone },\n      { name: 'Location', filled: !!resume.location },\n      { name: 'Summary', filled: !!resume.summary && resume.summary.length > 50 },\n      { name: 'Experience', filled: resume.experience && resume.experience.length > 0 },\n      { name: 'Education', filled: resume.education && resume.education.length > 0 },\n      { name: 'Skills', filled: resume.skills && resume.skills.length > 0 },\n    ];\n\n    const filledFields = fields.filter((f) => f.filled).length;\n    const totalFields = fields.length;\n    const missingFields = fields.filter((f) => !f.filled).map((f) => f.name);\n\n    return {\n      percentage: Math.round((filledFields / totalFields) * 100),\n      filledFields,\n      totalFields,\n      missingFields,\n    };\n  },\n\n  getValidationSummary(results: ValidationResult[]): {\n    errors: number;\n    warnings: number;\n    valid: number;\n    bySection: Record<string, { errors: number; warnings: number }>;\n  } {\n    const bySection: Record<string, { errors: number; warnings: number }> = {};\n\n    results.forEach((result) => {\n      const section = result.field.split('[')[0].split('.')[0];\n      if (!bySection[section]) {\n        bySection[section] = { errors: 0, warnings: 0 };\n      }\n      if (result.status === 'error') bySection[section].errors++;\n      if (result.status === 'warning') bySection[section].warnings++;\n    });\n\n    return {\n      errors: results.filter((r) => r.status === 'error').length,\n      warnings: results.filter((r) => r.status === 'warning').length,\n      valid: results.filter((r) => r.status === 'valid').length,\n      bySection,\n    };\n  },\n};\n\nexport default validationService;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":30,"oldLines":7,"newStart":30,"newLines":7,"lines":["     const activeTemplate = template || banner will remain visible\n    } finally {","originalFile":"import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\n\ninterface DatabaseSharingConfig {\n  sharedPath: string;\n  isConfigured: boolean;\n  exporterName: string;\n}\n\ninterface SnapshotInfo {\n  filename: string;\n  exportedAt: string;\n  exportedBy: string;\n  sizeBytes: number;\n  recordCounts: Record<string, number>;\n  isNew: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface SnapshotsResponse {\n  snapshots: SnapshotInfo[];\n}\n\ninterface ImportResponse {\n  success: boolean;\n}\n\nconst CONFIG_ENDPOINT = '/api/database/config';\nconst SNAPSHOTS_ENDPOINT = '/api/database/snapshots';\nconst IMPORT_ENDPOINT = '/api/database/import';\n\nconst getSnapshotKey = (snapshot: SnapshotInfo) => `${snapshot.filename}:${snapshot.exportedAt}`;\n\nconst formatDate = (isoDate: string) =>\n  new Date(isoDate).toLocaleString(undefined, {\n    year: 'numeric',\n    month: 'short',\n    day: '2-digit',\n    hour: '2-digit',\n    minute: '2-digit',\n  });\n\nconst formatRecordSummary = (recordCounts: Record<string, number>) => {\n  const tableCount = Object.keys(recordCounts).length;\n  const totalRecords = Object.values(recordCounts).reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0);\n  if (tableCount === 0) return '0 records';\n  if (tableCount === 1) return `${totalRecords.toLocaleString()} record${totalRecords === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;\n  return `${totalRecords.toLocaleString()} records across ${tableCount} tables`;\n};\n\nexport default function DatabaseUpdateBanner() {\n  const [isConfigured, setIsConfigured] = useState(false);\n  const [latestNewSnapshot, setLatestNewSnapshot] = useState<SnapshotInfo | null>(null);\n  const [dismissedSnapshotKey, setDismissedSnapshotKey] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [isImporting, setIsImporting] = useState(false);\n\n  const loadBannerState = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const configResponse = await fetch(CONFIG_ENDPOINT);\n      if (!configResponse.ok) {\n        setIsConfigured(false);\n        setLatestNewSnapshot(null);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const config = (await configResponse.json()) as DatabaseSharingConfig;\n      if (!config.isConfigured) {\n        setIsConfigured(false);\n        setLatestNewSnapshot(null);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      setIsConfigured(true);\n\n      const snapshotsResponse = await fetch(SNAPSHOTS_ENDPOINT);\n      if (!snapshotsResponse.ok) {\n        setLatestNewSnapshot(null);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const data = (await snapshotsResponse.json()) as SnapshotsResponse;\n      const newestSnapshot = data.snapshots\n        .filter((snapshot) => snapshot.isNew)\n        .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.exportedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.exportedAt).getTime())[0] ?? null;\n\n      setLatestNewSnapshot(newestSnapshot);\n    } catch {\n      setLatestNewSnapshot(null);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    void loadBannerState();\n    const intervalId = window.setInterval(() => {\n      void loadBannerState();\n    }, 60000);\n\n    return () => window.clearInterval(intervalId);\n  }, [loadBannerState]);\n\n  const recordSummary = useMemo(\n    () => (latestNewSnapshot ? formatRecordSummary(latestNewSnapshot.recordCounts) : ''),\n    [latestNewSnapshot]\n  );\n\n  if (!isConfigured || !latestNewSnapshot) return null;\n\n  const snapshotKey = getSnapshotKey(latestNewSnapshot);\n  if (dismissedSnapshotKey === snapshotKey) return null;\n\n  const handleDismiss = () => {\n    setDismissedSnapshotKey(snapshotKey);\n  };\n\n  const handleImport = async () => {\n    if (isImporting) return;\n    setIsImporting(true);\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(IMPORT_ENDPOINT, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ filename: latestNewSnapshot.filename }),\n      });\n\n      if (!response.ok) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const result = (await response.json()) as ImportResponse;\n      if (result.success) {\n        setDismissedSnapshotKey(snapshotKey);\n      }\n      await loadBannerState();\n    } catch {\n    } finally {\n      setIsImporting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"px-4 md:px-6 pt-4\">\n      <div className=\"glass-card p-4 border border-amber-200/60 dark:border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-50/60 dark:bg-amber-500/10\">\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-start gap-3\">\n            <div className=\"w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-accent-100/80 dark:bg-accent-500/20 flex items-center justify-center mt-0.5\">\n              <svg Do not rewrite or improve the content — extract it as-is from the source. Return no markdown code blocks, no explanations, just the raw JSON object.\n\nSchema:\n{\n  \"candidateName\": \"string\",\n  \"email\": \"string or null\",\n  \"phone\": \"string or null\",\n  \"location\": \"string or null\",\n  \"linkedIn\": \"string or null\",\n  \"summary\": \"string\",\n  \"experience\": [\n    {\n      \"company\": \"string\",\n      \"title\": \"string\",\n      \"projectName\": \"string or null\",\n      \"startDate\": \"string\",\n      \"endDate\": \"string\",\n      \"location\": \"string or null\",\n      \"description\": \"3-5 line paragraph describing the work, team, methodologies, and responsibilities\",\n      \"achievements\": [\"string\"],\n      \"technologies\": [\"string — each individual technology or tool used in this role\"]\n    }\n  ],\n  \"education\": [\n    {\n      \"institution\": \"string\",\n      \"degree\": \"string\",\n      \"field\": \"string\",\n      \"graduationDate\": \"string\",\n      \"gpa\": \"string or omit if not present\",\n      \"honors\": \"string or omit if not present\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"skills\": [\n    {\n      \"name\": \"string (category name, e.g. Technical Skills)\",\n      \"skills\": [\"string\"]\n    }\n  ],\n  \"certifications\": [\n    {\n      \"name\": \"string\",\n      \"issuer\": \"string\",\n      \"date\": \"string\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n\nIMPORTANT: For skills and technologies, split compound entries. E.g. 'Entity Framework/Dapper/ADO.NET' should become three separate items: 'Entity Framework', 'Dapper', 'ADO.NET'. Same for 'CI/CD (Jenkins, Git, JIRA)' → 'CI/CD', 'Jenkins', 'Git', 'JIRA'. Each skill/technology must be a single, atomic item.\nFor cloudSkills, classify skills related to AI, cloud platforms, or cloud-native tools. E.g. 'Azure CosmosDB', 'Azure Functions', 'AWS Lambda', 'OpenAI' are cloud/AI skills. Standard frameworks like 'React', '.NET', 'Entity Framework' are NOT cloud skills.\nFor each work experience entry, extract a 'technologies' array listing the individual technologies, frameworks, tools, and platforms mentioned. Split compound entries. Also extract 'projectName' if the resume mentions a specific project name for that role.\n\nResume text:\n${rawText}`;\n}\n\nasync function callClaudeLocal(config: AIConfig, prompt: string): Promise<{ content: string; usage: TokenUsage | null }> {\n  const baseUrl = config.localEndpoint || '/api/claude/v1';\n  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/chat/completions`, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model: config.model,\n      max_tokens: config.maxTokens,\n      temperature: config.temperature,\n      messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],\n    }),\n  });\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    const errorBody = await response.text();\n    throw new Error(`Proxy error ${response.status}: ${errorBody}`);\n  }\n  const data = await response.json();\n  const content = data.choices[0].message.content;\n  const rawUsage = data.usage;\n  const usage: TokenUsage | null = rawUsage\n    ? {\n        promptTokens: rawUsage.prompt_tokens ?? 0,\n        completionTokens: rawUsage.completion_tokens ?? 0,\n        totalTokens: rawUsage.total_tokens ?? 0,\n      }\n    : null;\n  return { content, usage };\n}\n\nfunction mapToStructuredResume(\n  parsed: Record<string, unknown>,\n  fileName: string,\n  fileType: 'pdf' | 'docx' | 'txt',\n  rawText: string\n): StructuredResume {\n  const now = Date.now();\n\n  const rawExperience = Array.isArray(parsed.experience) ? parsed.experience : [];\n  const rawEducation = Array.isArray(parsed.education) ? parsed.education : [];\n  const rawSkills = Array.isArray(parsed.skills) ? parsed.skills : [];\n  const rawCertifications = Array.isArray(parsed.certifications) ? parsed.certifications : [];\n\n  const experience: ExperienceEntry[] = rawExperience.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const e = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `exp-${now}-${idx}`,\n      company: String(e.company || ''),\n      title: String(e.title || ''),\n      startDate: String(e.startDate || ''),\n      endDate: String(e.endDate || ''),\n      location: e.location ? String(e.location) : undefined,\n      description: String(e.description || ''),\n      achievements: Array.isArray(e.achievements) ? e.achievements.map(String) : [],\n      technologies: Array.isArray(e.technologies) ? e.technologies.map(String) : [],\n      projectName: e.projectName ? String(e.projectName) : undefined,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const education: EducationEntry[] = rawEducation.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const e = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `edu-${now}-${idx}`,\n      institution: String(e.institution || ''),\n      degree: String(e.degree || ''),\n      field: String(e.field || ''),\n      graduationDate: String(e.graduationDate || ''),\n      gpa: e.gpa ? String(e.gpa) : undefined,\n      honors: e.honors ? String(e.honors) : undefined,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const skills: SkillCategory[] = rawSkills.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const s = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `skill-cat-${now}-${idx}`,\n      name: String(s.name || ''),\n      skills: Array.isArray(s.skills) ? s.skills.map(String) : [],\n    };\n  });\n\n  const certifications: CertificationEntry[] = rawCertifications.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const c = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `cert-${now}-${idx}`,\n      name: String(c.name || ''),\n      issuer: String(c.issuer || ''),\n      date: String(c.date || ''),\n    };\n  });\n\n  const processedSkills = splitCompoundSkills(skills);\n  const allExtractedSkills = processedSkills.flatMap(cat => cat.skills);\n  const classified = classifyCloudSkills(allExtractedSkills);\n\n  return {\n    id: `resume-${now}`,\n    originalFileName: fileName,\n    originalFileType: fileType,\n    originalContent: rawText,\n    candidateName: String(parsed.candidateName || ''),\n    email: parsed.email ? String(parsed.email) : undefined,\n    phone: parsed.phone ? String(parsed.phone) : undefined,\n    location: parsed.location ? String(parsed.location) : undefined,\n    linkedIn: parsed.linkedIn ? String(parsed.linkedIn) : undefined,\n    summary: String(parsed.summary || ''),\n    experience,\n    education,\n    skills: processedSkills,\n    certifications,\n    templateSkills: classified.techSkills,\n    cloudSkills: classified.cloudSkills,\n    status: 'transformed',\n    transformedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    validationResults: [],\n    overallValidationStatus: 'pending',\n  };\n}\n\nfunction splitCompoundSkills(skills: SkillCategory[]): SkillCategory[] {\n  return skills.map(cat => ({\n    ...cat,\n    skills: cat.skills.flatMap(skill => {\n      let parts = [skill];\n      if (skill.includes('/') && !skill.match(/^\\.NET|^Node\\.js|^CI\\/CD/i)) {\n        parts = skill.split('/').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n      }\n      return parts.flatMap(part => {\n        const match = part.match(/^(.+?)\\s*\\(([^)]+)\\)$/);\n        if (match) {\n          const base = match[1].trim();\n          const inner = match[2].split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n          return [base, ...inner];\n        }\n        return [part];\n      });\n    }),\n  }));\n}\n\nfunction classifyCloudSkills(allSkills: string[]): { techSkills: string[]; cloudSkills: string[] } {\n  const cloudPatterns = /azure|aws|gcp|cloud|lambda|cosmos|openai|gemini|anthropic|vertex|sagemaker|bedrock|devops/i;\n  const genericExclusions = /^azure\\s*hosting$/i;\n\n  const cloudSkills = allSkills.filter(s => cloudPatterns.test(s) && !genericExclusions.test(s));\n  const cloudSet = new Set(cloudSkills);\n  const techSkills = allSkills.filter(s => !cloudSet.has(s)).slice(0, TECH_SKILL_SLOTS);\n  return { techSkills, cloudSkills };\n}\n\nfunction regexFallbackParse(\n  rawText: string,\n  fileName: string,\n  fileType: 'pdf' | 'docx' | 'txt'\n): StructuredResume {\n  const sectionKeywords = [\n    'WORK EXPERIENCE', 'EXPERIENCE', 'EMPLOYMENT',\n    'EDUCATION', 'SKILLS', 'TECHNICAL SKILLS',\n    'CERTIFICATIONS', 'SUMMARY', 'PROFILE',\n    'OBJECTIVE', 'ABOUT', 'TECHNOLOGIES'\n  ];\n  const inlineHeaderPattern = new RegExp(\n    `(?<=[.!?\\\\s])\\\\s*(${sectionKeywords.join('|')})\\\\b`, 'gi'\n  );\n  const normalizedText = rawText.replace(inlineHeaderPattern, '\\n$1');\n\n  const now = Date.now();\n\n  const headerPattern = /^\\s*(?:EXPERIENCE|WORK EXPERIENCE|EMPLOYMENT|EDUCATION|SKILLS|TECHNICAL SKILLS|CERTIFICATIONS|SUMMARY|PROFILE|OBJECTIVE|ABOUT)/im;\n\n  const lines = normalizedText.split('\\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n  const firstHeaderIdx = lines.findIndex((l) => headerPattern.test(l));\n  const candidateNameRaw = firstHeaderIdx > 0\n    ? lines.slice(0, firstHeaderIdx).find((l) => l.length > 1 && l.length < 80) || lines[0] || ''\n    : lines[0] || '';\n  const candidateName = candidateNameRaw.length > 60\n    ? candidateNameRaw.substring(0, (candidateNameRaw.indexOf(' ', 25) + 1) || 60).trim()\n    : candidateNameRaw;\n\n  const emailMatch = rawText.match(/[\\w.-]+@[\\w.-]+\\.\\w+/);\n  const email = emailMatch?.[0];\n\n  const phoneMatch = rawText.match(/(?:\\+?\\d[\\d\\s().-]{8,}\\d)/);\n  const phone = phoneMatch?.[0]?.trim();\n\n  const sectionSplitter = /\\n\\s*(?=(?:EXPERIENCE|WORK EXPERIENCE|EMPLOYMENT|EDUCATION|SKILLS|TECHNICAL SKILLS|CERTIFICATIONS|SUMMARY|PROFILE|OBJECTIVE|ABOUT)\\b)/im;\n  const sections = normalizedText.split(sectionSplitter);\n\n  const findSection = (...keywords: string[]): string => {\n    const pattern = new RegExp(`^\\\\s*(?:${keywords.join('|')})`, 'im');\n    return sections.find((s) => pattern.test(s)) || '';\n  };\n\n  const summarySection = findSection('SUMMARY', 'PROFILE', 'OBJECTIVE', 'ABOUT');\n  const summaryText = summarySection\n    .replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '')\n    .trim()\n    .split('\\n')\n    .filter(Boolean)\n    .join(' ')\n    .substring(0, 600);\n\n  const experienceSection = findSection('EXPERIENCE', 'WORK EXPERIENCE', 'EMPLOYMENT');\n  const experienceLines = experienceSection.replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '').trim().split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  const dateRangePattern = /(?:(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec)\\w*\\s+\\d{4}|\\d{4})\\s*[-–—]\\s*(?:Present|(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec)\\w*\\s+\\d{4}|\\d{4})/i;\n\n  const experience: ExperienceEntry[] = [];\n  let currentExpLines: string[] = [];\n  let expIdx = 0;\n\n  const flushExperience = () => {\n    if (currentExpLines.length === 0) return;\n    const block = currentExpLines.join('\\n');\n    const dateMatch = block.match(dateRangePattern);\n    const parts = dateMatch ? dateMatch[0].split(/[-–—]/).map((s) => s.trim()) : ['', ''];\n    const descLines = currentExpLines.filter((l) => !dateRangePattern.test(l));\n    experience.push({\n      id: `exp-${now}-${expIdx++}`,\n      company: descLines[0] || '',\n      title: descLines[1] || '',\n      startDate: parts[0] || '',\n      endDate: parts[1] || '',\n      description: descLines.slice(2).join(' ').substring(0, 500),\n      achievements: [],\n      technologies: [],\n      projectName: undefined,\n    });\n    currentExpLines = [];\n  };\n\n  for (const line of experienceLines) {\n    if (dateRangePattern.test(line) && currentExpLines.length > 0) {\n      currentExpLines.push(line);\n    } else if (line.length > 2 && line.length < 100 && /^[A-Z]/.test(line) && currentExpLines.length > 1) {\n      flushExperience();\n      currentExpLines.push(line);\n    } else {\n      currentExpLines.push(line);\n    }\n  }\n  flushExperience();\n\n  const educationSection = findSection('EDUCATION');\n  const educationLines = educationSection.replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '').trim().split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  const degreePattern = /\\b(?:Bachelor|Master|PhD|Doctor|BS|MS|BA|MA|B\\.S\\.|M\\.S\\.|B\\.A\\.|M\\.A\\.|Associate|Diploma)\\b/i;\n  const education: EducationEntry[] = [];\n  let eduIdx = 0;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < educationLines.length; i++) {\n    const line = educationLines[i];\n    if (degreePattern.test(line) || (i === 0 && line.length > 2)) {\n      const degreeMatch = line.match(/\\b(?:Bachelor[^\\n,]*|Master[^\\n,]*|PhD[^\\n,]*|Doctor[^\\n,]*|BS|MS|BA|MA|B\\.S\\.|M\\.S\\.|B\\.A\\.|M\\.A\\.|Associate[^\\n,]*|Diploma[^\\n,]*)/i);\n      const dateMatch = line.match(/\\d{4}/);\n      education.push({\n        id: `edu-${now}-${eduIdx++}`,\n        institution: educationLines[i - 1] || line,\n        degree: degreeMatch?.[0] || '',\n        field: line.replace(degreeMatch?.[0] || '', '').replace(/[,\\-]/g, '').trim().substring(0, 80),\n        graduationDate: dateMatch?.[0] || '',\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  const skillsSection = findSection('SKILLS', 'TECHNICAL SKILLS');\n  const skillsBody = skillsSection.replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '').trim();\n  const rawSkillItems = skillsBody.split(/[,|;•\\n]+/).map((s) => s.trim()).filter((s) => s.length > 1 && s.length < 60);\n  const skills: SkillCategory[] = rawSkillItems.length > 0\n    ? [{ id: `skill-cat-${now}-0`, name: 'Skills', skills: rawSkillItems }]\n    : [];\n\n  const processedSkills = splitCompoundSkills(skills);\n\n  const certSection = findSection('CERTIFICATIONS');\n  const certLines = certSection.replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '').trim().split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  const certKeywordPattern = /certif(?:ied|ication|icate)/i;\n  const certifications: CertificationEntry[] = certLines\n    .filter((l) => certKeywordPattern.test(l) || l.length > 5)\n    .slice(0, 10)\n    .map((l, idx) => {\n      const dateMatch = l.match(/\\d{4}/);\n      return {\n        id: `cert-${now}-${idx}`,\n        name: l.replace(/\\d{4}.*$/, '').trim(),\n        issuer: '',\n        date: dateMatch?.[0] || '',\n      };\n    });\n\n  const allProcessedSkills = processedSkills.flatMap(cat => cat.skills);\n  const classified = classifyCloudSkills(allProcessedSkills);\n\n  return {\n    id: `resume-${now}`,\n    originalFileName: fileName,\n    originalFileType: fileType,\n    originalContent: rawText,\n    candidateName,\n    email,\n    phone,\n    summary: summaryText || '',\n    experience,\n    education,\n    skills: processedSkills,\n    certifications,\n    templateSkills: classified.techSkills,\n    cloudSkills: classified.cloudSkills,\n    status: 'transformed',\n    transformedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    validationResults: [],\n    overallValidationStatus: 'pending',\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildEnhancementPrompt(resume: StructuredResume, mode: RefinementMode): string {\n  const modeInstructions: Record<RefinementMode, string> = {\n    'professional-polish': 'Refine the language to be more professional and polished. Use strong action verbs, eliminate filler words, and improve clarity.',\n    'impact-focused': 'Rewrite to emphasize measurable impact and achievements. Add quantified results where possible (percentages, dollar amounts, team sizes).',\n    'ats-optimized': 'Optimize for Applicant Tracking Systems. Use industry-standard keywords, remove creative formatting, and ensure keyword density.',\n    'job-tailoring': 'Enhance the content to be more compelling and tailored for the target role.',\n  };\n\n  const allSkills = resume.skills.flatMap(cat => cat.skills);\n\n  return `Enhance this resume content. Mode: ${mode}. Instructions: ${modeInstructions[mode]}\n\nReturn ONLY valid JSON (no markdown, no explanation) with this structure:\n{\n  \"summary\": \"enhanced summary text\",\n  \"experience\": [\n    { \"description\": \"enhanced description\", \"achievements\": [\"enhanced achievement 1\"], \"technologies\": [\"technology1\", \"technology2\"] }\n  ],\n  \"templateSkills\": [\"top 14 most relevant technical skills\"],\n  \"cloudSkills\": [\"all AI/cloud-related skills\"]\n}\n\nKeep the same number of experience entries in the same order. Only enhance summary and experience descriptions/achievements/technologies. When returning technologies, split compound entries (e.g. 'Entity Framework/Dapper' → separate items).\n\nCurrent resume data:\n${JSON.stringify({ summary: resume.summary, experience: resume.experience.map(e => ({ description: e.description, achievements: e.achievements, technologies: e.technologies || [] })) })}\nAlso select the TOP 14 most relevant technical skills and all AI/cloud tools from: ${JSON.stringify(allSkills)}`;\n}\n\nexport const aiService = {\n  getConfig(): AIConfig {\n    const stored = localStorage.getItem('ai_config');\n    if (stored) {\n      currentConfig = JSON.parse(stored);\n    }\n    return currentConfig;\n  },\n\n  async checkConnection(): Promise<boolean> {\n    const config = this.getConfig();\n    const baseUrl = config.localEndpoint || '/api/claude/v1';\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/models`, {\n        method: 'GET',\n        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),\n      });\n      return response.ok;\n    } catch {\n      return false;\n    }\n  },\n\n  updateConfig(config: Partial<AIConfig>): AIConfig {\n    currentConfig = { ...currentConfig, ...config };\n    localStorage.setItem('ai_config', JSON.stringify(currentConfig));\n    return currentConfig;\n  },\n\n  async extractResumeData(rawText: string, fileName: string): Promise<{ resume: StructuredResume; metrics: ResumeProcessingMetrics }> {\n    const config = this.getConfig();\n    const fileType = (fileName.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'txt') as 'pdf' | 'docx' | 'txt';\n    const startTime = performance.now();\n\n    try {\n      const prompt = buildExtractionPrompt(rawText);\n      const { content: response, usage } = await callClaudeLocal(config, prompt);\n      let cleanJson = response.trim();\n      if (cleanJson.startsWith('```')) {\n        cleanJson = cleanJson.replace(/^```(?:json)?\\n?/, '').replace(/```$/, '').trim();\n      }\n      const parsed = JSON.parse(cleanJson);\n      const resume = mapToStructuredResume(parsed, fileName, fileType, rawText);\n      const processingTimeMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startTime);\n      return { resume, metrics: { extractionTokens: usage, totalTokens: usage, processingTimeMs, modelUsed: config.model, wasAiExtraction: true } };\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn('AI extraction failed, using regex fallback:', error);\n      return { resume: regexFallbackParse(rawText, fileName, fileType), metrics: { extractionTokens: null, totalTokens: null, processingTimeMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startTime), modelUsed: config.model, wasAiExtraction: false } };\n    }\n  },\n\n  async generateSuggestions(\n    _text: string,\n    _sectionType: string,\n    _context?: string\n  ): Promise<SuggestionOption[]> {\n    throw new Error('Suggestions require AI connection');\n  },\n\n  async transformResume(\n    rawContent: string,\n    fileName: string,\n    _refinementMode?: RefinementMode,\n    _jobDescription?: string\n  ): Promise<{ resume: StructuredResume; metrics: ResumeProcessingMetrics }> {\n    return this.extractResumeData(rawContent, fileName);\n  },\n\n  async enhanceFullResume(resume: StructuredResume, mode: RefinementMode): Promise<{ resume: StructuredResume; usage: TokenUsage | null }> {\n    const config = this.getConfig();\n    const prompt = buildEnhancementPrompt(resume, mode);\n    const { content, usage } = await callClaudeLocal(config, prompt);\n    let cleanJson = content.trim();\n    if (cleanJson.startsWith('```')) {\n      cleanJson = cleanJson.replace(/^```(?:json)?\\n?/, '').replace(/```$/, '').trim();\n    }\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(cleanJson);\n    const enhanced = {\n      ...resume,\n      summary: parsed.summary || resume.summary,\n      templateSkills: parsed.templateSkills || resume.templateSkills,\n      cloudSkills: parsed.cloudSkills || resume.cloudSkills,\n      experience: resume.experience.map((exp, i) => ({\n        ...exp,\n        description: parsed.experience?.[i]?.description || exp.description,\n        achievements: parsed.experience?.[i]?.achievements || exp.achievements,\n        technologies: parsed.experience?.[i]?.technologies || exp.technologies || [],\n      })),\n    };\n    return { resume: enhanced, usage };\n  },\n\n  async rephraseText(_text: string, _style: 'professional' | 'concise' | 'detailed'): Promise<string> {\n    throw new Error('Rephrase requires AI connection');\n  },\n\n  async extendContent(_text: string): Promise<string> {\n    throw new Error('Extend requires AI connection');\n  },\n\n  async validateWithAI(_resume: StructuredResume): Promise<AISuggestion[]> {\n    throw new Error('AI validation requires AI connection');\n  },\n};\n\nexport default aiService;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":462,"oldLines":11,"newStart":462,"newLines":7,"lines":["     }","   },"," ","-  async generateSuggestions(","-    _text: string,","-    _sectionType: string,","-    _context?: string","-  ): Promise<SuggestionOption[]> {","+  async generateSuggestions(): Promise<SuggestionOption[]> {","     throw new Error('Suggestions require AI connection');","   },"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011Gj3TPmzGVngMQJsYyweZr","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/eduardo.torres/Downloads/COE Operation Nexus/.agent-studio/worktrees/electron-architect-t3/src/apps/resume/services/aiService.ts has been updated successfully."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bd155553-9be2-4bdf-b8e3-51c1bfa1fd83","uuid":"f1dca88f-9586-4d5a-ac4a-351cad4a0193","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/eduardo.torres/Downloads/COE Operation Nexus/.agent-studio/worktrees/electron-architect-t3/src/apps/resume/services/aiService.ts","oldString":"  async transformResume(\n    rawContent: string,\n    fileName: string,\n    _refinementMode?: RefinementMode,\n    _jobDescription?: string\n  ): Promise<{ resume: StructuredResume; metrics: ResumeProcessingMetrics }> {\n    return this.extractResumeData(rawContent, fileName);\n  },","newString":"  async transformResume(\n    rawContent: string,\n    fileName: string\n  ): Promise<{ resume: StructuredResume; metrics: ResumeProcessingMetrics }> {\n    return this.extractResumeData(rawContent, fileName);\n  },","originalFile":"import { AIConfig, AISuggestion, SuggestionOption, StructuredResume, RefinementMode, ExperienceEntry, EducationEntry, SkillCategory, CertificationEntry, TokenUsage, ResumeProcessingMetrics } from '../types';\n\nconst DEFAULT_AI_CONFIG: AIConfig = {\n  provider: 'local',\n  localEndpoint: '/api/claude/v1',\n  cloudEndpoint: 'https://api.anthropic.com/v1',\n  model: 'claude-sonnet-4',\n  temperature: 0.1,\n  maxTokens: 4096,\n};\n\nlet currentConfig: AIConfig = { ...DEFAULT_AI_CONFIG };\n\nconst TECH_SKILL_SLOTS = 14;\n\nfunction buildExtractionPrompt(rawText: string): string {\n  return `Extract the resume information from the text below and return ONLY valid JSON matching this exact schema. Do not rewrite or improve the content — extract it as-is from the source. Return no markdown code blocks, no explanations, just the raw JSON object.\n\nSchema:\n{\n  \"candidateName\": \"string\",\n  \"email\": \"string or null\",\n  \"phone\": \"string or null\",\n  \"location\": \"string or null\",\n  \"linkedIn\": \"string or null\",\n  \"summary\": \"string\",\n  \"experience\": [\n    {\n      \"company\": \"string\",\n      \"title\": \"string\",\n      \"projectName\": \"string or null\",\n      \"startDate\": \"string\",\n      \"endDate\": \"string\",\n      \"location\": \"string or null\",\n      \"description\": \"3-5 line paragraph describing the work, team, methodologies, and responsibilities\",\n      \"achievements\": [\"string\"],\n      \"technologies\": [\"string — each individual technology or tool used in this role\"]\n    }\n  ],\n  \"education\": [\n    {\n      \"institution\": \"string\",\n      \"degree\": \"string\",\n      \"field\": \"string\",\n      \"graduationDate\": \"string\",\n      \"gpa\": \"string or omit if not present\",\n      \"honors\": \"string or omit if not present\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"skills\": [\n    {\n      \"name\": \"string (category name, e.g. Technical Skills)\",\n      \"skills\": [\"string\"]\n    }\n  ],\n  \"certifications\": [\n    {\n      \"name\": \"string\",\n      \"issuer\": \"string\",\n      \"date\": \"string\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n\nIMPORTANT: For skills and technologies, split compound entries. E.g. 'Entity Framework/Dapper/ADO.NET' should become three separate items: 'Entity Framework', 'Dapper', 'ADO.NET'. Same for 'CI/CD (Jenkins, Git, JIRA)' → 'CI/CD', 'Jenkins', 'Git', 'JIRA'. Each skill/technology must be a single, atomic item.\nFor cloudSkills, classify skills related to AI, cloud platforms, or cloud-native tools. E.g. 'Azure CosmosDB', 'Azure Functions', 'AWS Lambda', 'OpenAI' are cloud/AI skills. Standard frameworks like 'React', '.NET', 'Entity Framework' are NOT cloud skills.\nFor each work experience entry, extract a 'technologies' array listing the individual technologies, frameworks, tools, and platforms mentioned. Split compound entries. Also extract 'projectName' if the resume mentions a specific project name for that role.\n\nResume text:\n${rawText}`;\n}\n\nasync function callClaudeLocal(config: AIConfig, prompt: string): Promise<{ content: string; usage: TokenUsage | null }> {\n  const baseUrl = config.localEndpoint || '/api/claude/v1';\n  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/chat/completions`, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model: config.model,\n      max_tokens: config.maxTokens,\n      temperature: config.temperature,\n      messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],\n    }),\n  });\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    const errorBody = await response.text();\n    throw new Error(`Proxy error ${response.status}: ${errorBody}`);\n  }\n  const data = await response.json();\n  const content = data.choices[0].message.content;\n  const rawUsage = data.usage;\n  const usage: TokenUsage | null = rawUsage\n    ? {\n        promptTokens: rawUsage.prompt_tokens ?? 0,\n        completionTokens: rawUsage.completion_tokens ?? 0,\n        totalTokens: rawUsage.total_tokens ?? 0,\n      }\n    : null;\n  return { content, usage };\n}\n\nfunction mapToStructuredResume(\n  parsed: Record<string, unknown>,\n  fileName: string,\n  fileType: 'pdf' | 'docx' | 'txt',\n  rawText: string\n): StructuredResume {\n  const now = Date.now();\n\n  const rawExperience = Array.isArray(parsed.experience) ? parsed.experience : [];\n  const rawEducation = Array.isArray(parsed.education) ? parsed.education : [];\n  const rawSkills = Array.isArray(parsed.skills) ? parsed.skills : [];\n  const rawCertifications = Array.isArray(parsed.certifications) ? parsed.certifications : [];\n\n  const experience: ExperienceEntry[] = rawExperience.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const e = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `exp-${now}-${idx}`,\n      company: String(e.company || ''),\n      title: String(e.title || ''),\n      startDate: String(e.startDate || ''),\n      endDate: String(e.endDate || ''),\n      location: e.location ? String(e.location) : undefined,\n      description: String(e.description || ''),\n      achievements: Array.isArray(e.achievements) ? e.achievements.map(String) : [],\n      technologies: Array.isArray(e.technologies) ? e.technologies.map(String) : [],\n      projectName: e.projectName ? String(e.projectName) : undefined,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const education: EducationEntry[] = rawEducation.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const e = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `edu-${now}-${idx}`,\n      institution: String(e.institution || ''),\n      degree: String(e.degree || ''),\n      field: String(e.field || ''),\n      graduationDate: String(e.graduationDate || ''),\n      gpa: e.gpa ? String(e.gpa) : undefined,\n      honors: e.honors ? String(e.honors) : undefined,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const skills: SkillCategory[] = rawSkills.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const s = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `skill-cat-${now}-${idx}`,\n      name: String(s.name || ''),\n      skills: Array.isArray(s.skills) ? s.skills.map(String) : [],\n    };\n  });\n\n  const certifications: CertificationEntry[] = rawCertifications.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const c = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `cert-${now}-${idx}`,\n      name: String(c.name || ''),\n      issuer: String(c.issuer || ''),\n      date: String(c.date || ''),\n    };\n  });\n\n  const processedSkills = splitCompoundSkills(skills);\n  const allExtractedSkills = processedSkills.flatMap(cat => cat.skills);\n  const classified = classifyCloudSkills(allExtractedSkills);\n\n  return {\n    id: `resume-${now}`,\n    originalFileName: fileName,\n    originalFileType: fileType,\n    originalContent: rawText,\n    candidateName: String(parsed.candidateName || ''),\n    email: parsed.email ? String(parsed.email) : undefined,\n    phone: parsed.phone ? String(parsed.phone) : undefined,\n    location: parsed.location ? String(parsed.location) : undefined,\n    linkedIn: parsed.linkedIn ? String(parsed.linkedIn) : undefined,\n    summary: String(parsed.summary || ''),\n    experience,\n    education,\n    skills: processedSkills,\n    certifications,\n    templateSkills: classified.techSkills,\n    cloudSkills: classified.cloudSkills,\n    status: 'transformed',\n    transformedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    validationResults: [],\n    overallValidationStatus: 'pending',\n  };\n}\n\nfunction splitCompoundSkills(skills: SkillCategory[]): SkillCategory[] {\n  return skills.map(cat => ({\n    ...cat,\n    skills: cat.skills.flatMap(skill => {\n      let parts = [skill];\n      if (skill.includes('/') && !skill.match(/^\\.NET|^Node\\.js|^CI\\/CD/i)) {\n        parts = skill.split('/').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n      }\n      return parts.flatMap(part => {\n        const match = part.match(/^(.+?)\\s*\\(([^)]+)\\)$/);\n        if (match) {\n          const base = match[1].trim();\n          const inner = match[2].split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n          return [base, ...inner];\n        }\n        return [part];\n      });\n    }),\n  }));\n}\n\nfunction classifyCloudSkills(allSkills: string[]): { techSkills: string[]; cloudSkills: string[] } {\n  const cloudPatterns = /azure|aws|gcp|cloud|lambda|cosmos|openai|gemini|anthropic|vertex|sagemaker|bedrock|devops/i;\n  const genericExclusions = /^azure\\s*hosting$/i;\n\n  const cloudSkills = allSkills.filter(s => cloudPatterns.test(s) && !genericExclusions.test(s));\n  const cloudSet = new Set(cloudSkills);\n  const techSkills = allSkills.filter(s => !cloudSet.has(s)).slice(0, TECH_SKILL_SLOTS);\n  return { techSkills, cloudSkills };\n}\n\nfunction regexFallbackParse(\n  rawText: string,\n  fileName: string,\n  fileType: 'pdf' | 'docx' | 'txt'\n): StructuredResume {\n  const sectionKeywords = [\n    'WORK EXPERIENCE', 'EXPERIENCE', 'EMPLOYMENT',\n    'EDUCATION', 'SKILLS', 'TECHNICAL SKILLS',\n    'CERTIFICATIONS', 'SUMMARY', 'PROFILE',\n    'OBJECTIVE', 'ABOUT', 'TECHNOLOGIES'\n  ];\n  const inlineHeaderPattern = new RegExp(\n    `(?<=[.!?\\\\s])\\\\s*(${sectionKeywords.join('|')})\\\\b`, 'gi'\n  );\n  const normalizedText = rawText.replace(inlineHeaderPattern, '\\n$1');\n\n  const now = Date.now();\n\n  const headerPattern = /^\\s*(?:EXPERIENCE|WORK EXPERIENCE|EMPLOYMENT|EDUCATION|SKILLS|TECHNICAL SKILLS|CERTIFICATIONS|SUMMARY|PROFILE|OBJECTIVE|ABOUT)/im;\n\n  const lines = normalizedText.split('\\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n  const firstHeaderIdx = lines.findIndex((l) => headerPattern.test(l));\n  const candidateNameRaw = firstHeaderIdx > 0\n    ? lines.slice(0, firstHeaderIdx).find((l) => l.length > 1 && l.length < 80) || lines[0] || ''\n    : lines[0] || '';\n  const candidateName = candidateNameRaw.length > 60\n    ? candidateNameRaw.substring(0, (candidateNameRaw.indexOf(' ', 25) + 1) || 60).trim()\n    : candidateNameRaw;\n\n  const emailMatch = rawText.match(/[\\w.-]+@[\\w.-]+\\.\\w+/);\n  const email = emailMatch?.[0];\n\n  const phoneMatch = rawText.match(/(?:\\+?\\d[\\d\\s().-]{8,}\\d)/);\n  const phone = phoneMatch?.[0]?.trim();\n\n  const sectionSplitter = /\\n\\s*(?=(?:EXPERIENCE|WORK EXPERIENCE|EMPLOYMENT|EDUCATION|SKILLS|TECHNICAL SKILLS|CERTIFICATIONS|SUMMARY|PROFILE|OBJECTIVE|ABOUT)\\b)/im;\n  const sections = normalizedText.split(sectionSplitter);\n\n  const findSection = (...keywords: string[]): string => {\n    const pattern = new RegExp(`^\\\\s*(?:${keywords.join('|')})`, 'im');\n    return sections.find((s) => pattern.test(s)) || '';\n  };\n\n  const summarySection = findSection('SUMMARY', 'PROFILE', 'OBJECTIVE', 'ABOUT');\n  const summaryText = summarySection\n    .replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '')\n    .trim()\n    .split('\\n')\n    .filter(Boolean)\n    .join(' ')\n    .substring(0, 600);\n\n  const experienceSection = findSection('EXPERIENCE', 'WORK EXPERIENCE', 'EMPLOYMENT');\n  const experienceLines = experienceSection.replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '').trim().split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  const dateRangePattern = /(?:(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec)\\w*\\s+\\d{4}|\\d{4})\\s*[-–—]\\s*(?:Present|(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec)\\w*\\s+\\d{4}|\\d{4})/i;\n\n  const experience: ExperienceEntry[] = [];\n  let currentExpLines: string[] = [];\n  let expIdx = 0;\n\n  const flushExperience = () => {\n    if (currentExpLines.length === 0) return;\n    const block = currentExpLines.join('\\n');\n    const dateMatch = block.match(dateRangePattern);\n    const parts = dateMatch ? dateMatch[0].split(/[-–—]/).map((s) => s.trim()) : ['', ''];\n    const descLines = currentExpLines.filter((l) => !dateRangePattern.test(l));\n    experience.push({\n      id: `exp-${now}-${expIdx++}`,\n      company: descLines[0] || '',\n      title: descLines[1] || '',\n      startDate: parts[0] || '',\n      endDate: parts[1] || '',\n      description: descLines.slice(2).join(' ').substring(0, 500),\n      achievements: [],\n      technologies: [],\n      projectName: undefined,\n    });\n    currentExpLines = [];\n  };\n\n  for (const line of experienceLines) {\n    if (dateRangePattern.test(line) && currentExpLines.length > 0) {\n      currentExpLines.push(line);\n    } else if (line.length > 2 && line.length < 100 && /^[A-Z]/.test(line) && currentExpLines.length > 1) {\n      flushExperience();\n      currentExpLines.push(line);\n    } else {\n      currentExpLines.push(line);\n    }\n  }\n  flushExperience();\n\n  const educationSection = findSection('EDUCATION');\n  const educationLines = educationSection.replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '').trim().split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  const degreePattern = /\\b(?:Bachelor|Master|PhD|Doctor|BS|MS|BA|MA|B\\.S\\.|M\\.S\\.|B\\.A\\.|M\\.A\\.|Associate|Diploma)\\b/i;\n  const education: EducationEntry[] = [];\n  let eduIdx = 0;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < educationLines.length; i++) {\n    const line = educationLines[i];\n    if (degreePattern.test(line) || (i === 0 && line.length > 2)) {\n      const degreeMatch = line.match(/\\b(?:Bachelor[^\\n,]*|Master[^\\n,]*|PhD[^\\n,]*|Doctor[^\\n,]*|BS|MS|BA|MA|B\\.S\\.|M\\.S\\.|B\\.A\\.|M\\.A\\.|Associate[^\\n,]*|Diploma[^\\n,]*)/i);\n      const dateMatch = line.match(/\\d{4}/);\n      education.push({\n        id: `edu-${now}-${eduIdx++}`,\n        institution: educationLines[i - 1] || line,\n        degree: degreeMatch?.[0] || '',\n        field: line.replace(degreeMatch?.[0] || '', '').replace(/[,\\-]/g, '').trim().substring(0, 80),\n        graduationDate: dateMatch?.[0] || '',\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  const skillsSection = s.trim()).filter((s) => s.length > 1 && s.length < 60);\n  const skills: SkillCategory[] = rawSkillItems.length > 0\n    ? [{ id: `skill-cat-${now}-0`, name: 'Skills', skills: rawSkillItems }]\n    : [];\n\n  const processedSkills = splitCompoundSkills(skills);\n\n  const certSection = findSection('CERTIFICATIONS');\n  const certLines = certSection.replace(/^[^\\n]*\\n/, '').trim().split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  const certKeywordPattern = /certif(?:ied|ication|icate)/i;\n  const certifications: CertificationEntry[] = certLines\n    .filter((l) => certKeywordPattern.test(l) || l.length > 5)\n    .slice(0, 10)\n    .map((l, idx) => {\n      const dateMatch = l.match(/\\d{4}/);\n      return {\n        id: `cert-${now}-${idx}`,\n        name: l.replace(/\\d{4}.*$/, '').trim(),\n        issuer: '',\n        date: dateMatch?.[0] || '',\n      };\n    });\n\n  const allProcessedSkills = processedSkills.flatMap(cat => cat.skills);\n  const classified = classifyCloudSkills(allProcessedSkills);\n\n  return {\n    id: `resume-${now}`,\n    originalFileName: fileName,\n    originalFileType: fileType,\n    originalContent: rawText,\n    candidateName,\n    email,\n    phone,\n    summary: summaryText || '',\n    experience,\n    education,\n    skills: processedSkills,\n    certifications,\n    templateSkills: classified.techSkills,\n    cloudSkills: classified.cloudSkills,\n    status: 'transformed',\n    transformedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    validationResults: [],\n    overallValidationStatus: 'pending',\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildEnhancementPrompt(resume: StructuredResume, mode: RefinementMode): string {\n  const modeInstructions: Record<RefinementMode, string> = {\n    'professional-polish': 'Refine the language to be more professional and polished. Add quantified results where possible (percentages, dollar amounts, team sizes).',\n    'ats-optimized': 'Optimize for Applicant Tracking Systems. Mode: ${mode}. Instructions: ${modeInstructions[mode]}\n\nReturn ONLY valid JSON (no markdown, no explanation) with this structure:\n{\n  \"summary\": \"enhanced summary text\",\n  \"experience\": [\n    { \"description\": \"enhanced description\", \"achievements\": [\"enhanced achievement 1\"], \"technologies\": [\"technology1\", \"technology2\"] }\n  ],\n  \"templateSkills\": [\"top 14 most relevant technical skills\"],\n  \"cloudSkills\": [\"all AI/cloud-related skills\"]\n}\n\nKeep the same number of experience entries in the same order. Only enhance summary and experience descriptions/achievements/technologies. When returning technologies, split compound entries (e.g. 'Entity Framework/Dapper' → separate items).\n\nCurrent resume data:\n${JSON.stringify({ summary: resume.summary, experience: resume.experience.map(e => ({ description: e.description, achievements: e.achievements, technologies: e.technologies || [] })) })}\nAlso select the TOP 14 most relevant technical skills and all AI/cloud tools from: ${JSON.stringify(allSkills)}`;\n}\n\nexport const aiService = {\n  getConfig(): AIConfig {\n    const stored = localStorage.getItem('ai_config');\n    if (stored) {\n      currentConfig = JSON.parse(stored);\n    }\n    return currentConfig;\n  },\n\n  async checkConnection(): Promise<boolean> {\n    const config = this.getConfig();\n    const baseUrl = config.localEndpoint || '/api/claude/v1';\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/models`, {\n        method: 'GET',\n        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),\n      });\n      return response.ok;\n    } catch {\n      return false;\n    }\n  },\n\n  updateConfig(config: Partial<AIConfig>): AIConfig {\n    currentConfig = { ...currentConfig, ...config };\n    localStorage.setItem('ai_config', JSON.stringify(currentConfig));\n    return currentConfig;\n  },\n\n  async extractResumeData(rawText: string, fileName: string): Promise<{ resume: StructuredResume; parsed.certifications : [];\n\n  const experience: ExperienceEntry[] = rawExperience.map((item: unknown, idx: number) => {\n    const e = (item as Record<string, unknown>) || {};\n    return {\n      id: `exp-${now}-${idx}`,\n      company: String(e.company || ''),\n      title: String(e.title || ''),\n      startDate: String(e.startDate || ''),\n      endDate: String(e.endDate || ''),\n      location: e.location ? String(e.location) : undefined,\n      description: String(e.description || ''),\n      achievements: Array.isArray(e.achievements) ? e.achievements.map(String) : [],\n      technologies: Array.isArray(e.technologies) ? e.technologies.map(String) : [],\n      projectName: e.projectName ?
